Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on p-adic structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 679. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 135 with relations in degree 4.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 679 + O(1/n^135). Summing over all modes and applying the p-adic convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
